HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002","root_001533/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_216ea578d701c9720b66","text":"3. **An inner horizon that can open like dawn.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ف س/B009` is dawn or day opening and spreading like breath; 91:8 `ف ج ر/B002` is dawn breaking from night. **Structural cue and role:** day manifests in 91:3, night covers in 91:4, the self is shaped, and then `فجورها` repeats the dawn-root; the breath-opening branch lets the self internalize the cosmic alternation. **Abductive move:** the lexical recurrence is supplied, but reading the self as an inner horizon is analogical. **Reading change:** `سواها` can include opening an interior space in which alternatives break into awareness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001132/B002","root_001533/B001","root_001533/B009","root_001559/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26e8ec59f1f883c3f30c","text":"2. **Breath/opening connects soul to dawn and day.** Lexical evidence: ن ف س B001 \"breath leaving the body\"; ن ف س B009 \"opening or spreading like breath\"; ف ج ر B002 \"dawn breaking from night\" in 91:8; ن ه ر B002 \"day opening with light\" in 91:3. Structural cue: the soul comes after cosmic opening images and before فجور/تقوى. Assigned role: نفس is read as living breath that opens, making moral inspiration an inward dawn. Abductive move: I supply the metaphor that inner discernment opens like breath/daybreak; the packet supplies breath/opening and dawn/day branches. Reading change: \"and the soul\" becomes respiratory and luminous: the self is a place where dawn-like distinctions can break open.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000476/B001","root_001533/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_28c07fdf6175327fc283","text":"3. **Inner mind as the target of inspired intake.** Lexical evidence: ن ف س B013 \"inner mind, thought, or hidden intent\"; ل ه م B001 \"swallowing and taking something in completely\" in 91:8; د س و B001 hidden insertion in 91:10. Structural cue: \"what proportioned it\" is followed by \"then inspired it.\" Assigned role: the inner mind is the vessel; ilham is the intake; dass is a rival illicit insertion. Abductive move: I infer that inspiration is an inward placing/uptake rather than only external instruction; the packet supplies inner thought, swallowing intake, and hidden insertion. Reading change: the ayah prepares the self as a receptive interior whose moral knowledge can be taken in or buried.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_28f85a15031e8928d614","text":"2. **A calibrated arena for opposed capacities.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `س و ي/B001` is equality or equivalence and `س و ي/B006` is a middle or fair position. **Structural cue and role:** the paired objects in 91:8 and the balanced success/failure clauses in 91:9-10 surround the same feminine suffix, assigning `سواها` the role of calibration. **Abductive move:** the syntactic pairings are packet-given; inferring that the self is proportioned to hold both possibilities in accountable relation is mine. **Reading change:** shaping becomes moral calibration rather than only bodily perfection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B002","root_000766/B006","root_001533/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2e397aa674289f2d77dd","text":"1. **The self as fashioned equilibrium within cosmic measures.** Lexical evidence: ن ف س B011 \"living soul-self\"; س و ي B002 \"internal straightness and sound completion\"; س و ي B006 \"middle, fairness, and an even meeting place.\" Structural cue: after sky built and earth spread, the soul is made even; then inspiration divides فجور/تقوى. Assigned role: سوى supplies balanced constitution before moral alternatives appear. Abductive move: I infer that the later dual moral disclosure depends on an already proportioned inner structure; the packet supplies building/spreading/fashioning sequence. Reading change: the ayah makes the self a deliberately balanced site, not a chaotic interior suddenly receiving commands.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000766/B002","root_000766/B006","root_001533/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_362c9e6e4f187e2fe934","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:7:1\",\"root_001533\",[\"B013\"]],[\"91:7:3\",\"root_000766\",[\"B002\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Can, karşıt ahlaki imkânları ayırt edip sorumluluk içinde taşıyabilecek biçimde dengelenmiş ve tamamlanmış bir iç yeti olarak **Exploratory: the self as the stake in a contest, but not a game of chance.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ن ف س/B016` names a gambling arrow, while 91:2 `ق م ر/B007` carries gambling and contest; 91:9-10 then present gain and loss around the same self. **Structural cue and role:** these two dormant branches sit at the edges of the focus window and the paired outcomes make the self the contested object. **Abductive move:** the morphological distance from ordinary `نفس` to `النافس` is the weak link; the later agentive verbs `زكاها` and `دساها` correct any implication of randomness. **Reading change:** tentatively, the self appears as what is at stake in a consequential contest whose result is produced by action, not Structural cue: the repeated pronoun `ها` finally lands on نفس after cosmic referents. Assigned role: نفس B012 concentrates the prior \"her\" into an individuated essence. Abductive move: I infer that the cosmic pronoun-chain narrows into selfhood; the packet supplies repeated feminine pronouns and the root branch for essence.
